{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"exp-026: Feature Subset Stability Ablation\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nimport warnings\\nwarnings.filterwarnings(\\\"ignore\\\")\\n\\nimport json\\nimport numpy as np\\nimport pandas as pd\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\nfrom sklearn.model_selection import StratifiedKFold\\nfrom sklearn.metrics import roc_auc_score\\nimport lightgbm as lgb\\n\\nALL_FEAT_COLS = [\\n    \\\"surp_mean\\\", \\\"surp_std\\\", \\\"surp_skew\\\", \\\"surp_kurt\\\",\\n    \\\"surp_d1_mean\\\", \\\"surp_d1_std\\\", \\\"surp_d1_skew\\\",\\n    \\\"surp_d2_mean\\\", \\\"surp_d2_std\\\",\\n    \\\"ttr\\\", \\\"hapax_ratio\\\", \\\"self_bleu\\\",\\n    \\\"freq_kurtosis\\\", \\\"freq_entropy\\\", \\\"low_freq_ratio\\\",\\n]\\nTOP3_FEATURES = [\\\"surp_d1_std\\\", \\\"surp_std\\\", \\\"surp_d2_std\\\"]\\nTHETA = 0.60\\nSEED = 42\\nDATA_PATH = Path(\\\"/root/autodl-tmp/gen-depth-contamination/data/exp_008_retrain_chain/features.csv\\\")\\nOUT_PATH = Path(\\\"/root/autodl-tmp/gen-depth-contamination/artifacts/exp026_feature_stability.json\\\")\\n\\nSUBSET_CONFIGS = [\\n    (15, 1),\\n    (10, 5),\\n    (8, 5),\\n    (5, 5),\\n    (3, 1),\\n]\\n\\n\\ndef main():\\n    print(\\\"Loading data...\\\")\\n    df = pd.read_csv(DATA_PATH)\\n    df[ALL_FEAT_COLS] = df[ALL_FEAT_COLS].fillna(df[ALL_FEAT_COLS].mean())\\n    print(f\\\"Loaded {len(df)} rows, depths: {sorted(df['depth'].unique())}\\\")\\n\\n    rng = np.random.default_rng(SEED)\\n    all_indices = list(range(len(ALL_FEAT_COLS)))\\n    results = []\\n\\n    for subset_size, n_trials in SUBSET_CONFIGS:\\n        print(f\\\"\\\\n--- subset_size={subset_size}, n_trials={n_trials} ---\\\")\\n        \\n        if subset_size == 15:\\n            subsets = [all_indices]\\n        elif subset_size == 3:\\n            subsets = [[ALL_FEAT_COLS.index(f) for f in TOP3_FEATURES]]\\n        else:\\n            subsets = [sorted(rng.choice(all_indices, size=subset_size, replace=False).tolist()) for _ in range(n_trials)]\\n\\n        trial_results = []\\n        for i, feat_idx in enumerate(subsets):\\n            feat_names = [ALL_FEAT_COLS[j] for j in feat_idx]\\n            pairwise = {}\\n            for k in range(1, 5):\\n                pair_df = df[df[\\\"depth\\\"].isin([k-1, k])]\\n                X = pair_df[feat_names].values\\n                y = (pair_df[\\\"depth\\\"].values == k).astype(int)\\n                \\n                skf = StratifiedKFold(n_splits=5, shuffle=True, random_state=SEED)\\n                fold_aucs = []\\n                for train_idx, test_idx in skf.split(X, y):\\n                    clf = lgb.LGBMClassifier(\\n                        n_estimators=100, max_depth=5, learning_rate=0.1,\\n                        num_leaves=31, verbose=-1, n_jobs=4,\\n                    )\\n                    clf.fit(X[train_idx], y[train_idx])\\n                    proba = clf.predict_proba(X[test_idx])[:, 1]\\n                    fold_aucs.append(roc_auc_score(y[test_idx], proba))\\n                pairwise[f\\\"{k-1}v{k}\\\"] = float(np.mean(fold_aucs))\\n\\n            kstar = 0\\n            for k in range(1, 5):\\n                if pairwise[f\\\"{k-1}v{k}\\\"] > THETA:\\n                    kstar = k\\n                else:\\n                    break\\n\\n            trial_results.append({\\n                \\\"features\\\": feat_names,\\n                \\\"pairwise_auc\\\": pairwise,\\n                \\\"kstar\\\": kstar,\\n                \\\"auc_1v2\\\": pairwise[\\\"1v2\\\"],\\n            })\\n            print(f\\\"  trial {i}: K*={kstar}, 1v2={pairwise['1v2']:.4f}, 0v1={pairwise['0v1']:.4f}\\\")\\n\\n        num_kstar2 = sum(1 for t in trial_results if t[\\\"kstar\\\"] == 2)\\n        auc_1v2_vals = [t[\\\"auc_1v2\\\"] for t in trial_results]\\n        entry = {\\n            \\\"subset_size\\\": subset_size,\\n            \\\"num_tested\\\": len(subsets),\\n            \\\"num_kstar_2\\\": num_kstar2,\\n            \\\"min_1v2_auc\\\": round(min(auc_1v2_vals), 4),\\n            \\\"mean_1v2_auc\\\": round(float(np.mean(auc_1v2_vals)), 4),\\n \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-13T11:34:44.605Z\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"Frontier LLMs (GPT-5.5, Claude Opus 4.6) cannot reliably estimate generational depth via zero-shot prompting, motivating Binary human/AI detection is high (~88%) but fine-grained depth discrimination fails.\\\"}, \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\"}, \\\"exp_013_cross_transfer\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"created_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-12T20:50:57.813Z\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-13T06:54:11.689Z\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"Depth signal 是文本固有属性还是 model self-familiarity artifact？4×4 cross-model transfer matrix 测试分类器跨模型泛化能力\\\", \\\"metrics\\\": {\\\"accuracy_matrix\\\": {\\\"Qwen_rw→Qwen_rw\\\": 0.4229, \\\"Qwen_rw→Pythia\\\": 0.259, \\\"Qwen_rw→OLMo\\\": 0.2653, \\\"Qwen_rw→Qwen_cont\\\": 0.3466, \\\"Pythia→Qwen_rw\\\": 0.2718, \\\"Pythia→Pythia\\\": 0.3856, \\\"Pythia→OLMo\\\": 0.3531, \\\"Pythia→Qwen_cont\\\": 0.3287, \\\"OLMo→Qwen_rw\\\": 0.2768, \\\"OLMo→Pythia\\\": 0.345, \\\"OLMo→OLMo\\\": 0.3822, \\\"OLMo→Qwen_cont\\\": 0.3244, \\\"Qwen_cont→Qwen_rw\\\": 0.344, \\\"Qwen_cont→Pythia\\\": 0.3284, \\\"Qwen_cont→OLMo\\\": 0.3112, \\\"Qwen_cont→Qwen_cont\\\": 0.3875}, \\\"transfer_retention_accuracy\\\": {\\\"Pythia↔OLMo\\\": \\\"91-92%\\\", \\\"Qwen_rw→Pythia/OLMo\\\": \\\"61-63%\\\", \\\"Qwen_cont↔Pythia\\\": \\\"83-85%\\\", \\\"Qwen_cont↔OLMo\\\": \\\"80-85%\\\"}, \\\"transfer_retention_tau\\\": {\\\"Pythia↔OLMo\\\": \\\"85-86%\\\", \\\"Qwen_rw→Pythia/OLMo\\\": \\\"45-50%\\\", \\\"Qwen_cont↔others\\\": \\\"68-83%\\\"}, \\\"mean_pw_auc_matrix\\\": {\\\"within_avg\\\": 0.6099, \\\"cross_avg\\\": 0.5905, \\\"0v1_cross_all_above\\\": 0.96}, \\\"key_finding\\\": \\\"depth signal ≈ 70-85% text-intrinsic + 15-30% model self-familiarity. Binary contamination detection (0v1) highly transferable (>0.96 AUC). Fine-grained depth transfer depends on model family similarity. Qwen-rewrite overfits style features; Qwen-cont transfers much better.\\\"}, \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"depth signal ≈ 70-85% text-intrinsic + 15-30% model self-familiarity. Pythia↔OLMo 91-92% retention (architecture-agnostic). Qwen-rewrite catastrophic transfer to other models (61-63%), but Qwen-cont much better (80-85%). 0v1 universally transferable (>0.96 AUC), 1v2+ transfer depends on model family. Rewrite mode overfits style features → continuation mode is canonical condition for cross-model claims.\\\"}, \\\"exp_010_ordinal_baseline\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"created_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-13T04:22:42.199Z\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-13T04:26:54.571Z\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"Ordinal structure improves depth classification: ordinal methods should outperform unordered multiclass on MAE\\\", \\\"metrics\\\": {\\\"qwen_rewrite\\\": {\\\"obd_lgbm\\\": {\\\"accuracy\\\": 0.4229, \\\"mae\\\": 0.9836, \\\"tau_b\\\": 0.5518, \\\"mean_pw_auc\\\": 0.6214}, \\\"multiclass_lgbm\\\": {\\\"accuracy\\\": 0.4182, \\\"mae\\\": 1.0163, \\\"tau_b\\\": 0.5367, \\\"mean_pw_auc\\\": 0.6246}, \\\"ordinal_logreg\\\": {\\\"accuracy\\\": 0.3636, \\\"mae\\\": 0.9105, \\\"tau_b\\\": 0.563, \\\"mean_pw_auc\\\": 0.5022}}, \\\"pythia\\\": {\\\"obd_lgbm\\\": {\\\"accuracy\\\": 0.3856, \\\"mae\\\": 1.1072, \\\"tau_b\\\": 0.4745, \\\"mean_pw_auc\\\": 0.6055}, \\\"multiclass_lgbm\\\": {\\\"accuracy\\\": 0.3832, \\\"mae\\\": 1.1399, \\\"tau_b\\\": 0.4622, \\\"mean_pw_auc\\\": 0.6062}, \\\"ordinal_logreg\\\": {\\\"accuracy\\\": 0.3179, \\\"mae\\\": 1.022, \\\"tau_b\\\": 0.49, \\\"mean_pw_auc\\\": 0.4767}}, \\\"olmo\\\": {\\\"obd_lgbm\\\": {\\\"accuracy\\\": 0.3822, \\\"mae\\\": 1.1469, \\\"tau_b\\\": 0.4683, \\\"mean_pw_auc\\\": 0.6015}, \\\"multiclass_lgbm\\\": {\\\"accuracy\\\": 0.3685, \\\"mae\\\": 1.1813, \\\"tau_b\\\": 0.4497, \\\"mean_pw_auc\\\": 0.5974}, \\\"ordinal_logreg\\\": {\\\"accuracy\\\": 0.3265, \\\"mae\\\": 1.0352, \\\"tau_b\\\": 0.4656, \\\"mean_pw_auc\\\": 0.4859}}}, \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"Ordinal methods beat multiclass LightGBM on MAE across all 3 datasets. Best MAE consistently from mord LogisticAT, not OBD-LightGBM. However, ordinal_logreg has worst pairwise AUC — it reduces MAE by collapsing predictions toward the center, not by better discrimination.\\\"}, \\\"exp_001_pythia_depth\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"created_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-12T20:06:23.803Z\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-13T00:16:16.359Z\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"K* 跨模型一致性验证：Pythia-1.4B 在相同种子数据和解码策略下的 K* 是否与 Qwen2.5-1.5B 一致（K*=2）\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\", \\\"metrics\\\": {\\\"pairwise_auc\\\": {\\\"0v1\\\": 0.9932, \\\"1v2\\\": 0.6539, \\\"2v3\\\": 0.5515, \\\"3v4\\\": 0.5153, \\\"4v5\\\": 0.5135}, \\\"pairwise_auc_std\\\": {\\\"0v1\\\": 0.0007, \\\"1v2\\\": 0.0069, \\\"2v3\\\": 0.0137, \\\"3v4\\\": 0.0084, \\\"4v5\\\": 0.0078}, \\\"pairwise_jsd\\\": {\\\"0v1\\\": 0.2142, \\\"1v2\\\": 0.014, \\\"2v3\\\": 0.0051, \\\"3v4\\\": 0.0033, \\\"4v5\\\": 0.0031}, \\\"k_star\\\": 2, \\\"ordinal_mae\\\": 1.14, \\\"ordinal_accuracy\\\": 0.383}}, \\\"mvp_pilot_sat_v1\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"created_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-12T11:09:39.193Z\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-12T19:25:33.763Z\\\", \\\"hypothesis\\\": \\\"递归生成文本在统计特征空间上存在可分辨的代际差异，且差异随深度呈系统性趋势（K* ≥ 3）\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"v2 left-padding rerun, per-fold AUC, full self-BLEU\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"success\\\", \\\"metrics\\\": {\\\"pairwise_auc\\\": {\\\"0v1\\\": 0.9956, \\\"1v2\\\": 0.6957, \\\"2v3\\\": 0.5893, \\\"3v4\\\": 0.5434, \\\"4v5\\\": 0.5343}, \\\"pairwise_auc_std\\\": {\\\"0v1\\\": 0.0005, \\\"1v2\\\": 0.0072, \\\"2v3\\\": 0.012, \\\"3v4\\\": 0.0053, \\\"4v5\\\": 0.0091}, \\\"pairwise_jsd\\\": {\\\"0v1\\\": 0.2669, \\\"1v2\\\": 0.0163, \\\"2v3\\\": 0.0069, \\\"3v4\\\": 0.0047, \\\"4v5\\\": 0.0041}, \\\"k_star\\\": 2, \\\"ordinal_mae\\\": 1.0163, \\\"ordinal_accuracy\\\": 0.4182}}}, \\\"count\\\": 45, \\\"total\\\": 45, \\\"baseline\\\": null}, \\\"error\\\": null, \\\"metadata\\\": {\\\"elapsed_seconds\\\": 0.2292}}\"}",

      "result": "{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"experiments\": {\"exp_026_feature_stability\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"created_at\": \"2026-05-17T07:43:01.256Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-17T08:06:40.583Z\", \"hypothesis\": \"K*=2 对 feature 子集选择 robust：即使从 15D 特征中随机抽取 5/8/10 维子集，1v2 AUC 仍 > θ=0.60，证明 K* 边界不依赖特定特征组合（ad-hoc feature 防御）。\", \"parent\": \"exp_B_14d_ablation\", \"metrics\": {\"data_source\": \"exp_008_retrain_chain (K*=4)\", \"results\": {\"15D\": {\"num_tested\": 1, \"min_1v2_auc\": 0.9472, \"mean_1v2_auc\": 0.9472}, \"10D\": {\"num_tested\": 5, \"min_1v2_auc\": 0.8698, \"mean_1v2_auc\": 0.9202}, \"8D\": {\"num_tested\": 5, \"min_1v2_auc\": 0.8955, \"mean_1v2_auc\": 0.9156}, \"5D\": {\"num_tested\": 5, \"min_1v2_auc\": 0.8785, \"mean_1v2_auc\": 0.9081}, \"3D_top3\": {\"num_tested\": 1, \"min_1v2_auc\": 0.8314, \"mean_1v2_auc\": 0.8314}}, \"key_finding\": \"1v2 AUC >> 0.60 across all 17 feature subsets (min=0.8314 with top-3 only). Feature selection does not affect discriminability.\", \"note\": \"Data was retrain-chain (K*=4), not prompt-chain (K*=2). Should re-run on exp-001 data for K*=2 robustness verification.\"}, \"conclusion\": \"success\"}, \"exp_027_retrain_bootstrap\": {\"status\": \"PREPARING\", \"created_at\": \"2026-05-17T07:43:03.273Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-17T07:43:15.274Z\", \"hypothesis\": \"Retrain-chain 的 pairwise AUC（0v1 到 3v4）在 10000-iteration bootstrap 95% CI 下均显著高于 θ=0.60，确认 K*≥4 具有统计严格性。\", \"parent\": \"exp_008_retrain_chain_v2\"}, \"exp_009_dose_50pct_v3\": {\"status\": \"RUNNING\", \"created_at\": \"2026-05-17T00:13:08.251Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-17T07:05:49.067Z\", \"hypothesis\": \"50% synthetic injection 下 graduated filtering 应展现最大优势 >=2pp MMLU（v3: fix OOM, auto batch + offline）。半数数据为合成时 binary 激进丢弃导致数据饥饿。\", \"parent\": \"exp_009_dose_50pct_v2\"}, \"exp_009_dose_30pct_v3\": {\"status\": \"RUNNING\", \"created_at\": \"2026-05-17T00:13:06.603Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-17T07:05:47.756Z\", \"hypothesis\": \"30% synthetic injection 下 graduated filtering 优势应 >=1pp MMLU（v3: fix OOM, auto batch + offline）。更高污染率下 binary 丢弃更多可用 gen-1 数据。\", \"parent\": \"exp_009_dose_30pct_v2\"}, \"exp_009_dose_10pct_v3\": {\"status\": \"RUNNING\", \"created_at\": \"2026-05-17T00:13:04.692Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-17T07:05:46.004Z\", \"hypothesis\": \"10% synthetic injection 下 graduated filtering 应比 binary 保留 >=0.5pp MMLU（v3: fix OOM, auto batch + offline）。低污染时 gen-1 有训练价值，binary 全丢造成数据损失。\", \"parent\": \"exp_009_dose_10pct_v2\"}, \"exp_005_degradation\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"created_at\": \"2026-05-13T02:40:12.117Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-17T02:57:38.303Z\", \"metrics\": {\"cross_model_kendall_tau_by_first_auc\": {\"Pythia_vs_OLMo\": 0.733, \"Pythia_vs_Qwen_rewrite\": 0.467, \"Pythia_vs_Qwen_cont\": 0.695, \"OLMo_vs_Qwen_rewrite\": 0.429, \"OLMo_vs_Qwen_cont\": 0.543, \"Qwen_rewrite_vs_Qwen_cont\": 0.733}, \"cross_model_kendall_tau_by_avg_auc\": {\"Pythia_vs_OLMo\": 0.8476, \"Pythia_vs_Qwen_cont\": 0.7524, \"OLMo_vs_Qwen_cont\": 0.6762}, \"cross_model_spearman_rho_single_feature_auc\": {\"Pythia_vs_OLMo\": 0.95, \"Pythia_vs_Qwen_cont\": 0.9143, \"OLMo_vs_Qwen_cont\": 0.85, \"metric_definition\": \"Spearman ρ on per-feature AUC rankings (each feature independently classifies depth pairs)\"}, \"4model_tau_mean_continuation_by_first_auc\": 0.657, \"4model_tau_mean_all_by_first_auc\": 0.6, \"generation_mode_delta\": {\"Pythia_vs_Qwen\": \"+0.228\", \"OLMo_vs_Qwen\": \"+0.114\"}, \"top3_features_ranking_by_first_auc\": {\"Pythia\": [\"surp_d1_std\", \"surp_d2_std\", \"surp_std\"], \"OLMo\": [\"surp_std\", \"surp_d1_std\", \"surp_d2_std\"], \"Qwen_rewrite\": [\"freq_entropy\", \"surp_std\", \"surp_d1_std\"], \"Qwen_cont\": [\"surp_d1_std\", \"surp_std\", \"surp_d2_std\"]}, \"shared_top_discriminators\": \"surp_d1_std, surp_std, surp_d2_std shared across all architectures (single-feature AUC ranking)\", \"degradation_ordering_consistency\": \"Strong cross-model consistency (ρ≥0.85) on single-feature AUC ranking under controlled generation mode.\", \"residual_architecture_effect\": \"Qwen-cont vs OLMo (τ=0.54 by_first_auc) still lower than Pythia vs OLMo (τ=0.73), ~30-40% of ordering divergence from architecture\", \"limitation_1v2_weakness\": \"depth-pair level consistency weakest at 1v2 (τ~0.37), near-generational feature importance ordering varies more across models\"}, \"conclusion\": \"success\", \"notes\": \"4-model τ matrix complete. Generation mode confirmed as primary driver of Qwen divergence (Δτ=+0.23 for Pythia pair). Residual architecture effect ~30-40%. C2 claim: degradation ordering largely consistent under same generation mode, with noted architecture residuals.\", \"hypothesis\": \"递归生成文本在统计特征空间中呈现单调退化趋势（如 perplexity 上升、词汇多样性下降），且退化速率在 K*=2 后显著减缓，这解释了为什么分类器在深代后失去区分力。\", \"parent\": \"mvp_pilot_sat_v1\"}, \"exp_024_arxiv_domain\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"created_at\": \"2026-05-16T14:43:59.323Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-17T02:57:37.559Z\", \"hypothesis\": \"Domain ablation: arXiv abstracts 数据下 K*=2 是否成立？验证 K* 跨数据域（C4 vs Wikipedia vs arXiv）的稳定性。Qwen2.5-1.5B base, continuation, nucleus p=0.95 T=1.0, 5K docs, depth 0-5。\", \"notes\": \"2026-05-16 15:05 重启成功。PID 48295, cuda:2, 73% util。修复：depth_0.jsonl 已有 2200 行 arXiv 数据，prepare_seed_data 行数检查通过后跳过 C4 下载，HF_DATASETS_OFFLINE=1 不再冲突。n_docs=2200。预计 ~20 min 完成剩余 depth。\", \"metrics\": {\"K_star\": 3, \"pairwise_auc\": {\"0v1\": 0.9946, \"1v2\": 0.858, \"2v3\": 0.6427, \"3v4\": 0.5437, \"4v5\": 0.5294}, \"bootstrap_ci\": {\"0v1\": {\"auc\": 0.9946, \"ci_lower\": 0.9929, \"ci_upper\": 0.9961, \"perm_p\": 0.0001}, \"1v2\": {\"auc\": 0.858, \"ci_lower\": 0.848, \"ci_upper\": 0.8679, \"perm_p\": 0.0001}, \"2v3\": {\"auc\": 0.6427, \"ci_lower\": 0.6275, \"ci_upper\": 0.6575, \"perm_p\": 0.0001}, \"3v4\": {\"auc\": 0.5437, \"ci_lower\": 0.5276, \"ci_upper\": 0.5598, \"perm_p\": 0.0001}, \"4v5\": {\"auc\": 0.5294, \"ci_lower\": 0.5133, \"ci_upper\": 0.5455, \"perm_p\": 0.0006}}, \"bootstrap_resamples\": 10000, \"permutation_resamples\": 10000, \"key_finding\": \"arXiv domain K*=3 (CI-confirmed). 2v3 CI lower=0.6275 > θ=0.60. First condition with K*>2. 1v2 AUC=0.858 far exceeds C4/Wiki (~0.61-0.69).\"}, \"conclusion\": \"success\", \"parent\": \"exp_023_domain_ablation\"}, \"exp_017_qwen_continuation\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"created_at\": \"2026-05-12T20:41:22.423Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-17T02:57:37.021Z\", \"hypothesis\": \"Continuation mode 的 K* 应等于 rewrite mode（K*=2），因为 K* 饱和边界是递归生成的文本固有属性，与生成模式（续写 vs 改写）无关——两种模式在深代时都收敛到相同的统计吸引子。\", \"notes\": \"dry-run passed 2026-05-13: model OK (2.9G safetensors), env OK (torch=2.10.0+cu128, transformers=4.57.6), dirs auto-create. Submit command prepared. 等 exp-002 完成后立即提交。\", \"metrics\": {\"k_star\": 2, \"pairwise_auc\": {\"0v1\": 0.9972, \"1v2\": 0.6114, \"2v3\": 0.5609, \"3v4\": 0.5408, \"4v5\": 0.5139}, \"pairwise_jsd\": {\"0v1\": 0.28627, \"1v2\": 0.005107, \"2v3\": 0.003603, \"3v4\": 0.003395, \"4v5\": 0.003085}, \"ordinal_mae\": 1.1547, \"ordinal_accuracy\": 0.3819, \"top3_features\": [\"freq_entropy\", \"surp_mean\", \"surp_std\"]}, \"conclusion\": \"success\", \"parent\": \"mvp_pilot_sat_v1\"}, \"exp_012_binary_confidence\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"created_at\": \"2026-05-15T12:32:09.468Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-17T02:57:36.098Z\", \"notes\": \"N=500全量运行。Binary confidence与depth的Spearman ρ弱到中等(|ρ|=0.06-0.46)，6-class accuracy全部≤random baseline。关键发现：0v1 pairwise AUC极高(0.99+)但deeper pairs塌缩到~0.5，证明binary detector只捕捉human/AI边界，无法区分generational depth。Hypothesis验证成功。可视化已生成(exp012_confidence_vs_depth.pdf)。\", \"hypothesis\": \"Binary detector confidence（DivEye log-PPL、Binoculars ratio）与 true generational depth 相关性弱（Spearman rho < 0.3），因为这些检测器将所有合成文本折叠为单一类别，无法区分 gen-1 和 gen-3+，暴露 binary 范式的信息损失。\", \"conclusion\": \"success\", \"metrics\": {\"n_samples\": 500, \"generators\": [\"qwen-rewrite\", \"pythia-1.4b\", \"olmo-1b\", \"qwen-cont\"], \"6class_accuracy\": {\"qwen-rewrite\": 0.1987, \"pythia-1.4b\": 0.1303, \"olmo-1b\": 0.127, \"qwen-cont\": 0.1833, \"random_baseline\": 0.1667}, \"spearman_rho\": {\"qwen-rewrite\": {\"log_ppl_ref\": -0.1233, \"log_ppl_obs\": 0.0815, \"ppl_ratio\": 0.1185}, \"pythia-1.4b\": {\"log_ppl_ref\": -0.2972, \"log_ppl_obs\": -0.4529, \"ppl_ratio\": -0.392}, \"olmo-1b\": {\"log_ppl_ref\": -0.378, \"log_ppl_obs\": -0.4614, \"ppl_ratio\": -0.4068}, \"qwen-cont\": {\"log_ppl_ref\": -0.3887, \"log_ppl_obs\": -0.162, \"ppl_ratio\": -0.058}}, \"binary_detector_kstar\": 1, \"obd_kstar\": 2, \"auc_non_monotonic\": true, \"key_finding\": \"Binary detectors (DivEye/Binoculars) K*≈1 vs OBD K*=2. 6-class accuracy ≤0.199 (random=0.167). Spearman |ρ|=0.06-0.46 (statistically significant but practically weak). AUC non-monotonic — binary detectors cannot maintain depth ordering. Confirms binary confidence ≠ depth estimation.\"}, \"parent\": \"mvp_pilot_sat_v1\"}, \"exp_C_permutation_10k\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"created_at\": \"2026-05-15T11:18:26.152Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-17T02:57:35.128Z\", \"hypothesis\": \"10K permutation resamples 不应改变任何 K* 结论（所有 p < 0.01 的 pair 仍应 p < 0.01），确认 500 resamples 已有足够精度，K*=2 结论的统计基础稳固。\", \"conclusion\": \"success\", \"metrics\": {\"resamples\": 10000, \"all_boundary_pairs_p\": \"<0.0001\", \"key_finding\": \"10K permutation confirms all boundary p-values well below 0.01. FR-11 resolved.\"}, \"parent\": \"exp_007_bootstrap_ci\"}, \"exp_002_olmo_depth\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"created_at\": \"2026-05-12T20:06:24.766Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-17T02:57:34.160Z\", \"hypothesis\": \"K* 跨模型一致性验证：OLMo-1B 在相同种子数据和解码策略下的 K* 是否与 Qwen2.5-1.5B 一致（K*=2）\", \"metrics\": {\"k_star\": 2, \"pairwise_auc\": {\"0v1\": 0.997, \"1v2\": 0.6146, \"2v3\": 0.5497, \"3v4\": 0.509, \"4v5\": 0.5044}, \"pairwise_auc_std\": {\"0v1\": 0.0004, \"1v2\": 0.0029, \"2v3\": 0.004, \"3v4\": 0.0084, \"4v5\": 0.0136}, \"pairwise_jsd\": {\"0v1\": 0.23941, \"1v2\": 0.012972, \"2v3\": 0.00735, \"3v4\": 0.003968, \"4v5\": 0.003851}, \"ordinal_mae\": 1.1813, \"ordinal_accuracy\": 0.3685, \"top_features\": [\"surp_d1_mean\", \"surp_d1_skew\", \"freq_kurtosis\", \"surp_d2_mean\", \"surp_mean\"]}, \"conclusion\": \"success\", \"parent\": \"exp_001_pythia_depth\"}, \"exp_009_dose_50pct_v2\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"created_at\": \"2026-05-16T23:14:53.711Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-17T02:57:30.391Z\", \"hypothesis\": \"50% synthetic injection 下 graduated filtering 应展现最大优势 >=2pp MMLU（v2: fix HF mirror）。机制同 v1：半数数据为合成时 binary 激进丢弃导致数据饥饿。\", \"conclusion\": \"failed\", \"parent\": \"exp_009_dose_50pct\"}, \"exp_009_dose_30pct_v2\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"created_at\": \"2026-05-16T23:14:51.739Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-17T02:57:29.467Z\", \"hypothesis\": \"30% synthetic injection 下 graduated filtering 优势应 >=1pp MMLU（v2: fix HF mirror）。机制同 v1：更高污染率下 binary 丢弃更多可用 gen-1 数据。\", \"conclusion\": \"failed\", \"parent\": \"exp_009_dose_30pct\"}, \"exp_009_dose_10pct_v2\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"created_at\": \"2026-05-16T23:14:49.850Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-17T02:57:28.507Z\", \"hypothesis\": \"10% synthetic injection 下 graduated filtering 应比 binary 保留 >=0.5pp MMLU（v2: fix HF mirror）。机制同 v1：低污染时 gen-1 有训练价值，binary 全丢造成数据损失。\", \"conclusion\": \"failed\", \"parent\": \"exp_009_dose_10pct\"}, \"exp_009_dose_50pct\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"created_at\": \"2026-05-16T22:22:14.703Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-17T02:57:28.163Z\", \"hypothesis\": \"50% synthetic injection 下，graduated filtering 应展现最大优势（>=2pp MMLU），因为半数数据为合成时 binary filtering 的激进丢弃导致严重数据饥饿，而 graduated 保留 gen-1 维持训练集规模。\", \"conclusion\": \"failed\", \"parent\": \"exp_006_graduated_filtering\"}, \"exp_009_dose_30pct\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"created_at\": \"2026-05-16T22:22:12.817Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-17T02:57:27.096Z\", \"hypothesis\": \"30% synthetic injection 下，graduated filtering 优势应大于 10% 场景（>=1pp MMLU），因为更高污染率意味着 binary filtering 丢弃更多可用 gen-1 数据，而 graduated 仅移除 gen-2+ 有害部分。\", \"conclusion\": \"failed\", \"parent\": \"exp_006_graduated_filtering\"}, \"exp_009_dose_10pct\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"created_at\": \"2026-05-16T22:22:10.924Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-17T02:57:26.151Z\", \"hypothesis\": \"10% synthetic injection 下，graduated filtering 应比 binary filtering 保留更多 MMLU 性能（>=0.5pp），因为低污染场景中 gen-1 文本仍有训练价值，binary 全部丢弃造成不必要的数据损失。\", \"conclusion\": \"failed\", \"parent\": \"exp_006_graduated_filtering\"}, \"exp_kstar_theta\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"created_at\": \"2026-05-15T15:08:01.704Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-17T02:56:51.877Z\", \"hypothesis\": \"K*(θ) 曲线在 θ∈[0.55,0.65] 存在 plateau（K* 对阈值不敏感），且 Cohen's h 在 depth=K* 到 depth=K*+1 间降至 < 0.2（小效应量），为 θ=0.60 提供非任意性理论依据。\", ### Assistant

_source line 73_

完成。结果：

```
subset_size | num_tested | num_K*=2 | min_1v2_AUC | mean_1v2_AUC
15          | 1          | 1        | 0.6262      | 0.6262
10          | 5          | 5        | 0.6153      | 0.6191
8           | 5          | 5        | 0.6056      | 0.6159
5           | 5          | 2        | 0.5856      | 0.5998
3 (top-3)   | 1          | 0        | 0.5871      | 0.5871
```

关键发现：**Feature selection 在 K\*=2 边界处确实 matters。**

- **8D+ 子集 robust**：10D 和 8D 所有 5 次随机采样都 pass θ=0.60（min=0.6056）
- **5D 子集 fragile**：仅 2/5 pass θ，3 次掉到 0.585-0.594，mean=0.5998 刚好在边界
- **Top-3 特征 fail**：surp_d1_std + surp_std + surp_d2_std 只拿到 0.5871，不足以支撑 K\*=2

与 v1（retrain-chain, K\*=4）形成鲜明对比：v1 所有子集 trivially pass（min=0.8314）。数据源换对后，这个实验提供了论文需要的关键证据——在 K\* 边界处需要 ≥8D 特征才能稳健维持判别力。

Registry 和 artifact 均已更新。
